<script>
        function toggleRow(element) {
            const nextRow = element.nextElementSibling;
            if (nextRow && nextRow.classList.contains('expandable-row')) {
                const isExpanded = nextRow.classList.contains('expanded');
                
                // Close all other expandable rows if it's a table row
                if (element.tagName === 'TR') {
                     const allRows = element.closest('tbody').querySelectorAll('.expandable-row.expanded');
                     allRows.forEach(row => {
                         if (row !== nextRow) {
                             row.classList.remove('expanded');
                             row.previousElementSibling.classList.remove('bg-surface-variant/80');
                         }
                     });
                }
                
                nextRow.classList.toggle('expanded');
                
                if (nextRow.classList.contains('expanded')) {
                    element.classList.add('bg-surface-variant/80');
                } else {
                    element.classList.remove('bg-surface-variant/80');
                }
            } else {
                // Handle the agent ops div structure
                const expandable = element.querySelector('.expandable-row');
                if (expandable) {
                    expandable.classList.toggle('expanded');
                }
            }
        }
        
        // Add random delays to glitch animations
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
            const glitchEls = document.querySelectorAll('.glitch-anim');
            glitchEls.forEach((el, index) => {
                el.style.animationDelay = `${index * 0.15 + 0.1}s`;
            });
        });
    </script>

<!-- STITCH_THREEJS_START:ANIMATION_112 class="fixed inset-0 w-full h-full bg-transparent" -->
<div class="fixed inset-0 w-full h-full bg-transparent" style="display:block;">
<script src="https://ajax.googleapis.com/ajax/libs/threejs/r125/three.min.js"></script>
<div id="threejs-container-ANIMATION_112" style="width:100%;height:100%"></div>
<script>
(function() {
  const container = document.getElementById('threejs-container-ANIMATION_112');
  const devicePixelRatio = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
  const scene = new THREE.Scene();
const width = container.clientWidth || window.innerWidth;
const height = container.clientHeight || window.innerHeight;
const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(75, width / height, 0.1, 1000);
const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ alpha: true, antialias: true });
renderer.setSize(width, height);
container.appendChild(renderer.domElement);

// Create a representation of a 'Code Node'
const group = new THREE.Group();
const geometry = new THREE.IcosahedronGeometry(1, 1);
const material = new THREE.MeshPhongMaterial({
    color: 0x45cd8e,
    wireframe: true,
    transparent: true,
    opacity: 0.8,
    emissive: 0x45cd8e,
    emissiveIntensity: 0.5
});

const core = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.IcosahedronGeometry(0.5, 0), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x45cd8e, emissive: 0x45cd8e }));
group.add(core);

const shell = new THREE.Mesh(geometry, material);
group.add(shell);

// Orbiting particles
const particlesGeo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
const count = 100;
const posArray = new Float32Array(count * 3);
for(let i=0; i<count*3; i++) {
    posArray[i] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 5;
}
particlesGeo.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(posArray, 3));
const particlesMat = new THREE.PointsMaterial({ size: 0.02, color: 0x45cd8e });
const particles = new THREE.Points(particlesGeo, particlesMat);
group.add(particles);

scene.add(group);

const light = new THREE.PointLight(0x45cd8e, 2, 10);
light.position.set(2, 2, 2);
scene.add(light);
scene.add(new THREE.AmbientLight(0x404040));

camera.position.z = 5;

function animate() {
    requestAnimationFrame(animate);
    group.rotation.y += 0.005;
    group.rotation.x += 0.002;
    shell.scale.setScalar(1 + Math.sin(Date.now() * 0.002) * 0.05);
    renderer.render(scene, camera);
}

window.addEventListener('resize', () => {
    const w = container.clientWidth || window.innerWidth;
    const h = container.clientHeight || window.innerHeight;
    camera.aspect = w / h;
    camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
    renderer.setSize(w, h);
});

animate();
})();
</script>
</div>
<!-- STITCH_THREEJS_END:ANIMATION_112 -->
</body>
</html>


<!-- Shader -->
<!DOCTYPE html>

<html lang="en">
<head>
<meta charset="utf-8"/>
<meta content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1.0" name="viewport"/>
<script src="https://cdn.tailwindcss.com?plugins=forms,container-queries"></script>
<style>
    * { margin: 0; padding: 0; box-sizing: border-box; }
    html, body {
      width: 100%;
      height: 100%;
      overflow: hidden;
      background: #000;
    }
  </style>

<!-- STITCH_SHADER_START:ANIMATION_111 class="fixed inset-0 w-full h-full" -->
<div class="fixed inset-0 w-full h-full" style="display:block;">
<canvas id="shader-canvas-ANIMATION_111" style="display:block;width:100%;height:100%"></canvas>
<script>
(function() {
  const canvas = document.getElementById('shader-canvas-ANIMATION_111');

  // Sync the WebGL drawing-buffer size with the CSS-driven layout size.
  // This fires on initial layout and whenever the element is resized.
  function syncSize() {
    const w = canvas.clientWidth  || 1280;
    const h = canvas.clientHeight || 720;
    if (canvas.width !== w || canvas.height !== h) {
      canvas.width  = w;
      canvas.height = h;
    }
  }
  if (typeof ResizeObserver !== 'undefined') {
    new ResizeObserver(syncSize).observe(canvas);
  }
  syncSize();

  const gl = canvas.getContext('webgl') || canvas.getContext('experimental-webgl');
  if (!gl) return;
  const vs = `attribute vec2 a_position;
varying vec2 v_texCoord;
void main() {
  v_texCoord = a_position * 0.5 + 0.5;
  gl_Position = vec4(a_position, 0.0, 1.0);
}`;
  const fs = `precision highp float;
uniform float u_time;
uniform vec2 u_resolution;
uniform vec2 u_mouse;
varying vec2 v_texCoord;

float hash(vec2 p) {
    p = fract(p * vec2(123.34, 456.21));
    p += dot(p, p + 45.32);
    return fract(p.x * p.y);
}

void main() {
    vec2 uv = v_texCoord;
    vec2 st = uv * vec2(u_resolution.x/u_resolution.y, 1.0);
    
    // Core brand color from design system: #45cd8e -> vec3(0.27, 0.80, 0.56)
    vec3 colorA = vec3(0.05, 0.08, 0.07); // Dark background
    vec3 colorB = vec3(0.27, 0.80, 0.56); // Brand emerald
    
    // Telemetry pulse logic
    float grid = 40.0;
    vec2 grid_uv = floor(st * grid) / grid;
    float n = hash(grid_uv);
    
    float pulse = smoothstep(0.4, 0.6, sin(u_time * 0.5 + n * 10.0));
    float flow = smoothstep(0.98, 1.0, fract(st.y - u_time * 0.2 + n));
    
    vec3 finalColor = mix(colorA, colorB * 0.4, pulse * 0.2);
    finalColor += colorB * flow * 0.6;
    
    // Vignette
    float d = length(uv - 0.5);
    finalColor *= smoothstep(0.8, 0.4, d);
    
    gl_FragColor = vec4(finalColor, 1.0);
}`;
  function cs(type, src) {
    const s = gl.createShader(type);
    gl.shaderSource(s, src);
    gl.compileShader(s);
    return s;
  }
  const prog = gl.createProgram();
  gl.attachShader(prog, cs(gl.VERTEX_SHADER, vs));
  gl.attachShader(prog, cs(gl.FRAGMENT_SHADER, fs));
  gl.linkProgram(prog);
  gl.useProgram(prog);
  const buf = gl.createBuffer();
  gl.bindBuffer(gl.ARRAY_BUFFER, buf);
  gl.bufferData(gl.ARRAY_BUFFER, new Float32Array([-1,-1, 1,-1, -1,1, 1,1]), gl.STATIC_DRAW);
  const pos = gl.getAttribLocation(prog, 'a_position');
  gl.enableVertexAttribArray(pos);
  gl.vertexAttribPointer(pos, 2, gl.FLOAT, false, 0, 0);
  const uTime = gl.getUniformLocation(prog, 'u_time');
  const uRes = gl.getUniformLocation(prog, 'u_resolution');
  const uMouse = gl.getUniformLocation(prog, 'u_mouse');

  // u_mouse is in pixel coordinates matching u_resolution (ShaderToy convention).
  // Shaders that need normalized coords should use: u_mouse / u_resolution.
  let mouse = { x: canvas.width / 2, y: canvas.height / 2 };
  window.addEventListener('mousemove', (event) => {
    const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (rect.width && rect.height) {
      const nx = (event.clientX - rect.left) / rect.width;
      const ny = 1.0 - (event.clientY - rect.top) / rect.height;
      mouse.x = nx * canvas.width;
      mouse.y = ny * canvas.height;
    }
  });

  function render(t) {
    if (typeof ResizeObserver === 'undefined') syncSize();
    gl.viewport(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
    if (uTime) gl.uniform1f(uTime, t * 0.001);
    if (uRes) gl.uniform2f(uRes, canvas.width, canvas.height);
    if (uMouse) gl.uniform2f(uMouse, mouse.x, mouse.y);
    gl.drawArrays(gl.TRIANGLE_STRIP, 0, 4);
    requestAnimationFrame(render);
  }
  render(0);
})();
</script>
</div>
<!-- STITCH_SHADER_END:ANIMATION_111 -->
</body>
</html>

<script>
        function toggleRow(element) {
            const nextRow = element.nextElementSibling;
            if (nextRow && nextRow.classList.contains('expandable-row')) {
                nextRow.classList.toggle('expanded');
                // Optional: visually distinguish active row
                if (nextRow.classList.contains('expanded')) {
                    element.classList.add('bg-surface-variant');
                } else {
                    element.classList.remove('bg-surface-variant');
                }
            } else {
                // Handle the agent ops div structure
                const expandable = element.querySelector('.expandable-row');
                if (expandable) {
                    expandable.classList.toggle('expanded');
                }
            }
        }
    </script>

<div class="p-4 overflow-x-auto">
<pre class="font-mono-data text-mono-data leading-relaxed"><code class="text-ink-secondary"><span class="text-tertiary">import</span> requests
<span class="text-tertiary">from</span> core.circuit_breaker <span class="text-tertiary">import</span> Breaker

<span class="text-primary-fixed-dim">@Breaker</span>(timeout=2000, fallback=<span class="text-status-good-ink">"queue"</span>)
<span class="text-tertiary">class</span> <span class="text-ink-primary">SalesforceGateway</span>:
    <span class="text-tertiary">def</span> <span class="text-ink-primary">__init__</span>(self, config):
        self.endpoint = config.get(<span class="text-status-good-ink">'SF_ENDPOINT'</span>)
        <span class="text-[#787a79]"># Current timeout set to 5s. </span>
        <span class="text-[#787a79]"># Consider lowering to fail fast?</span>
        self.timeout = <span class="text-status-warning-ink">5.0</span>
        
    <span class="text-tertiary">def</span> <span class="text-ink-primary">sync_record</span>(self, payload):
        res = requests.post(
            self.endpoint, 
            json=payload,
            timeout=self.timeout
        )
        <span class="text-tertiary">return</span> res.json()</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<script>
        // Simple interaction to simulate log clicking focusing code
        document.querySelectorAll('.log-row').forEach(row => {
            row.addEventListener('click', function() {
                // Remove active class from all code rows
                document.querySelectorAll('.code-row').forEach(cr => {
                    cr.classList.remove('active', 'bg-status-critical-bg/10', 'bg-status-serious-bg/20');
                    cr.style.borderLeft = 'none';
                });
                
                // visual feedback on log
                this.style.backgroundColor = 'var(--surface-container-highest)';
                setTimeout(() => {
                    this.style.backgroundColor = '';
                }, 200);
            });
        });
    </script>

<div class="p-space-4 overflow-x-auto">
<pre class="font-code-md text-code-md leading-relaxed"><code class="text-text-secondary"><span class="text-primary-container">import</span> requests
<span class="text-primary-container">from</span> core.circuit_breaker <span class="text-primary-container">import</span> Breaker

<span class="text-primary-fixed">@Breaker</span>(timeout=2000, fallback=<span class="text-success">"queue"</span>)
<span class="text-primary-container">class</span> <span class="text-text-primary">SalesforceGateway</span>:
    <span class="text-primary-container">def</span> <span class="text-text-primary">__init__</span>(self, config):
        self.endpoint = config.get(<span class="text-success">'SF_ENDPOINT'</span>)
        <span class="text-text-secondary"># Current timeout set to 5s. </span>
        <span class="text-text-secondary"># Consider lowering to fail fast?</span>
        self.timeout = <span class="text-warning">5.0</span>
        
    <span class="text-primary-container">def</span> <span class="text-text-primary">sync_record</span>(self, payload):
        res = requests.post(
            self.endpoint, 
            json=payload,
            timeout=self.timeout
        )
        <span class="text-primary-container">return</span> res.json()</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px]" data-icon="manufacturing">manufacturing</span>

<!-- Left: Documentation Content (Markdown style) -->
<div class="flex-1 overflow-y-auto border-r border-border-muted">
<div class="max-w-3xl mx-auto py-space-6 px-space-6 pb-24">
<!-- Section Header -->
<div class="mb-space-6">
<div class="flex items-center space-x-2 mb-2">
<span class="bg-surface-container-highest border border-border-subtle text-text-secondary font-label-xs px-2 py-0.5 rounded-sm">REST API</span>
<span class="text-text-secondary font-label-xs">v2.0</span>
</div>
<h1 class="font-headline-lg text-headline-lg text-text-primary mb-2">Workflows</h1>
<p class="font-body-md text-text-secondary">
                        The Workflows API allows you to programmatically create, manage, and execute automation sequences within the Sync platform. Use these endpoints to integrate internal logic with external systems.
                    </p>
</div>
<!-- Endpoint: List Workflows -->
<div class="mb-12" id="list-workflows">
<h2 class="font-headline-md text-headline-md text-text-primary mb-4 border-b border-border-subtle pb-2">List all workflows</h2>
<p class="font-body-md text-text-secondary mb-4">
                        Returns a paginated list of all active workflows associated with your current environment.
                    </p>
<!-- Endpoint URL Block -->
<div class="bg-surface-card border border-border-subtle rounded-DEFAULT p-3 mb-6 flex items-center space-x-3">
<span class="bg-surface-variant text-info font-code-md px-2 py-1 rounded-sm text-xs border border-border-muted font-bold">GET</span>
<code class="font-code-md text-code-md text-text-primary flex-1">https://api.sync.dev/v2/workflows</code>
<button class="text-text-secondary hover:text-primary transition-colors" title="Copy URL">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-sm">content_copy</span>
</button>
</div>
<!-- Query Parameters Table -->
<h3 class="font-label-xs text-text-primary uppercase tracking-widest mb-3">Query Parameters</h3>
<div class="border border-border-subtle rounded-DEFAULT overflow-hidden mb-8">
<table class="w-full text-left border-collapse">
<thead>
<tr class="bg-surface-container-high border-b border-border-subtle">
<th class="py-2 px-3 font-label-xs text-text-secondary">Parameter</th>
<th class="py-2 px-3 font-label-xs text-text-secondary">Type</th>
<th class="py-2 px-3 font-label-xs text-text-secondary">Description</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody class="font-body-sm">
<tr class="border-b border-border-subtle">
<td class="py-3 px-3 align-top">
<code class="font-code-md text-text-primary bg-surface-variant px-1 rounded-sm border border-border-muted">limit</code>
</td>
<td class="py-3 px-3 align-top text-text-secondary font-code-md text-xs">integer</td>
<td class="py-3 px-3 align-top text-text-secondary">
                                        Maximum number of records to return. <br/>
<span class="text-xs mt-1 block">Default: <code class="font-code-md">20</code>, Max: <code class="font-code-md">100</code></span>
</td>
</tr>
<tr class="border-b border-border-subtle bg-surface-main/30">
<td class="py-3 px-3 align-top">
<code class="font-code-md text-text-primary bg-surface-variant px-1 rounded-sm border border-border-muted">status</code>
</td>
<td class="py-3 px-3 align-top text-text-secondary font-code-md text-xs">string</td>
<td class="py-3 px-3 align-top text-text-secondary">
                                        Filter by workflow status. Valid options are <code class="font-code-md text-primary">active</code>, <code class="font-code-md text-warning">paused</code>, or <code class="font-code-md text-error">failed</code>.
                                    </td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Endpoint: Create Workflow -->
<div class="mb-12" id="create-workflow">
<h2 class="font-headline-md text-headline-md text-text-primary mb-4 border-b border-border-subtle pb-2">Create a workflow</h2>
<p class="font-body-md text-text-secondary mb-4">
                        Creates a new automation workflow. You must provide a valid JSON configuration detailing the trigger and steps.
                    </p>
<!-- Endpoint URL Block -->
<div class="bg-surface-card border border-border-subtle rounded-DEFAULT p-3 mb-6 flex items-center space-x-3">
<span class="bg-surface-variant text-success font-code-md px-2 py-1 rounded-sm text-xs border border-border-muted font-bold">POST</span>
<code class="font-code-md text-code-md text-text-primary flex-1">https://api.sync.dev/v2/workflows</code>
</div>
<!-- Body Parameters Table -->
<h3 class="font-label-xs text-text-primary uppercase tracking-widest mb-3">Body Parameters</h3>
<div class="border border-border-subtle rounded-DEFAULT overflow-hidden mb-8">
<table class="w-full text-left border-collapse">
<thead>
<tr class="bg-surface-container-high border-b border-border-subtle">
<th class="py-2 px-3 font-label-xs text-text-secondary">Parameter</th>
<th class="py-2 px-3 font-label-xs text-text-secondary">Type</th>
<th class="py-2 px-3 font-label-xs text-text-secondary">Description</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody class="font-body-sm">
<tr class="border-b border-border-subtle">
<td class="py-3 px-3 align-top">
<div class="flex items-center space-x-2">
<code class="font-code-md text-text-primary bg-surface-variant px-1 rounded-sm border border-border-muted">name</code>
<span class="text-error text-[10px] font-bold uppercase">Required</span>
</div>
</td>
<td class="py-3 px-3 align-top text-text-secondary font-code-md text-xs">string</td>
<td class="py-3 px-3 align-top text-text-secondary">
                                        The display name of the workflow. Max 128 characters.
                                    </td>
</tr>
<tr class="border-b border-border-subtle bg-surface-main/30">
<td class="py-3 px-3 align-top">
<div class="flex items-center space-x-2">
<code class="font-code-md text-text-primary bg-surface-variant px-1 rounded-sm border border-border-muted">trigger_type</code>
<span class="text-error text-[10px] font-bold uppercase">Required</span>
</div>
</td>
<td class="py-3 px-3 align-top text-text-secondary font-code-md text-xs">string</td>
<td class="py-3 px-3 align-top text-text-secondary">
                                        Defines how the workflow initiates. Must be <code class="font-code-md">webhook</code>, <code class="font-code-md">schedule</code>, or <code class="font-code-md">event</code>.
                                    </td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
</div>
</div>
</div>
